LP implementation more details:
```
def lp_update(p, M, maxiter? ):
    n=len(p); m=2*n-1
    A = np.zeros((m,n))
    for j in range(m):
       lo=max(0,j-n+1); hi=min(n-1,j)
       # term: (p*q)_j = Σ_i p_i q_{j-i}; i range lo..hi, j-i index
       A[j, lo:hi+1] = p[hi:lo-1:-1]
    # constraints A q <= M. Scale: A/(M+eps) <=1
    res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A/M, b_ub=np.ones(m), bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    q=res.x
    if q.sum()>0: q/=q.sum()
    return q
```
Potential issue scaling M small. A/M values up to max(p)/M ~0.005/0.004=1.25, okay. If M=0 skip. If lp fails, return None.
Memory building A O(nm). For n up to 512, fine. For repeated calls, maybe rebuild. Could optimize with sparse `linear_operator`? scipy linprog highspy accepts dense. OK.

LP step could be expensive (~milliseconds to seconds). We can call say every 100-300 Adam steps or after stuck. But if it yields improvements, time worth. We can line search mix:

Adam optimizer implementation details:
```
def optimize_z(n, init_p, stop_time, seed, base_lr, max_starts, use_lp=True):
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    best_p=init_p.copy(); best_score=fft_score(best_p)
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    starts=0
    while time < stop_time- ...:
       z = log(init_p + eps) ; z -= mean
       # maybe random reinit if starts>0: add noise or use spectral/random
       p=softmax(z); c, score, U =...
       m,v zeros; t=0; tau_curr=...
       lr=base_lr
       no_improve=0; stuck=0
       while time < stop_start_deadline:
          # if very close to best? no
          # compute c maybe cached from p; update after linesearch
          # soft weights, U
          tau = max(tau_min, tau_scale * maxc * anneal)
          w, U = softmax(c, tau)
          g = 2*cross(p,w)
          # optional L2 reg: gL = 4*cross(c,p); g = alpha*g + reg*gL/(scale)
          gz = p*(g - dot(g,p))
          # Adam update m,v
          # direction = -mhat/sqrt(vhat); maybe normalize direction by max abs?
          # line search factors
          improved=False
          for factor in (1,0.5,...):
             nz = z - lr*factor*direction
             nz -= mean; normalize? softmax
             np_ = softmax(nz)
             cnp = conv(np_)
             cmax = cnp.max(); sc = 2*n*cmax
             # compute soft U_cand maybe reuse cnp and tau? If p changed, soft objective at same tau.
             # Accept if sc < cur_score or (U_cand < U and sc <= cur_score + tol)
          if improved: ...; if exact improves global best, update.
          else: lr *=0.8; stuck++; if stuck>threshold: break restart
       starts+=1
    return best
```
Need avoid recomputing c many times? We have cached c. For line search candidate, compute. For current after accept, carry c. For gradient, use current c/w.

Since c values ~0.004, tau 0.0004. Activations: weights for coefficients within tau of max. Good. We can also set tau floor `1e-6`.

Wait g=2 cross(p,w); weights sum 1, cross_i ≤? w probability over j, cross_i = Σ_l p_l w_{i+l}. Since sum over i cross_i = Σ_l p_l Σ_j w_j =1? Actually sum_i cross_i = Σ_l p_l Σ_j w_j =1. So cross magnitude vector L1=1, g L1=2. Soft grad O(1). L2 reg cross(c,p) L1 = sum_l p_l sum_j c_j =1, times4. So similar. We can set `reg` perhaps 0.05-0.1 initially to encourage flatness, later 0. How to compute softmax weights robust:
```
def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    m=c.max()
    x=(c-m)/tau
    exp= np.exp(np.clip(x, -50, 700))
    s=exp.sum()
    if s==0: uniform over ties
    w=exp/s
    U=m + tau*np.log(s)
```
Since c all nonnegative small; tau maybe small. Use all. For speed, if τ very small, we can restrict to top where x>-30; but full length small okay. For n=4096 length 8191, okay.

Cross-correlation function:
```
def cross_corr(p, w, L):
    P = fft(p, L) # zero pad
    W = fft(w, L) # w length m zero pad to L
    x = ifft(P * np.conj(W)).real[:n]
    return x
```
But recomputing FFT(W) each line search? Only current gradient. We compute once per iteration. For L2 reg, cross(c,p): We can compute cross(c,p) by same function with w=c; but c not probability (sum=1) so same. Need FFT of c; can compute. To save, we can combine gradients in frequency? g_soft =2 cross(p,w). g_L=4 cross(p,c). Through frequency: cross(p,w) = ifft(Pfft * conj(Wfft)); cross(p,c) = ifft(Pfft * conj(Cfft)). We can compute Pfft once, Cfft maybe from c? Note Cfft = FFT(c) length L. But Cfft = Pfft * Pfft? For full convolution zero-padded, c's DFT length L equals Pfft^2 (no wrap). Yes. So Cfft = Pfft*Pfft. Then conj(Cfft)=conj(Pfft)^2. cross(p,c)=ifft(Pfft * conj(Pfft)^2) = ifft(Pfft * conj(Pfft)^2). That's correlation of p with p*p. Could compute directly. But for L2, just call cross with c. `fft_score(p, L=None)`:
```

# maybe renormalize c sum to 1 to reduce numerical drift? If c sum deviates, scaling c would change max? But true c sum=1. If small error, rescale c by 1/sum? ```
```

`soft_stats(c, tau)`:
```

w=exp/s

```

`gradient_p(p, w, c, L, reg=0.0)`:
```
P=fft(p,L)
if reg==0:
   W=fft(w,L)
   cross=ifft(P*conj(W)).real[:n]
   g=2*cross
else:
   W=fft(w,L); C=fft(c,L)
   cross_w=ifft(P*conj(W)).real[:n]
   cross_c=ifft(P*conj(C)).real[:n]
   g=2*cross_w + reg*4*cross_c
```
But for c target L2, if we use `reg` units: g_total = 2 cross(p,w) + reg * 4 cross(p,c). If c sum=1, cross(c,p) L1=1, okay. Maybe use `reg` around 0.1. Weighted by score? Good.

LP update integration:
```
def lp_direction(p, score, n, L, stop_time):
    M=score/(2*n) # max coefficient
    if M<=0: return None
    # build A
    A = np.empty((m,n))
    # maybe use for j vectorized
    for j in range(m):
       lo = max(0, j-n+1); hi=min(n-1,j)
       A[j, lo:hi+1] = p[lo:hi+1][::-1]? Need A[row j, col i] = p_{j-i} for valid i. i ranges lo..hi. j-i ranges hi..lo? For i=lo, j-i=hi; i=hi, j-i=lo. So A[j, lo:hi+1] = p[hi:lo-1:-1]. yes.
    # constraints A q <= M
    # scale: A /= (M+1e-300)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A/M, b_ub=np.ones(m), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    if res x not finite or sum<=0 return None
    q=res.x; q=q/q.sum()
    return q
```
Build A each call O(nm). For n=256 okay. For many calls, maybe slow. We can build A via convolution of p? The matrix A is Toeplitz/Hankel band. Could use sparse but linprog dense. We can call rarely. To optimize, maybe only n<=512. For n>512 skip LP.

Potential issue: `linprog` may issue warnings; suppress. It may be slow if problem unbounded? no. We can set time limit? HiGHS no time limit option? Could pass `options={'time_limit': 2.0}`? HiGHS supports time_limit? maybe. Use try.

Detailed local optimizer:
```
def optimize_one(n, p0, stop_time, seed, use_lp=True, lp_interval=150):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    best_p = p0.copy(); best_sc = fft_score(best_p,L)
    z0 = log(p0+1e-12); z0-=mean; z0=clip(-20,20)
    # We can run until stop_time. But to allow multiple starts within run_length, pass subdeadline.
    while time < stop_time:
       z = z0.copy()
       # maybe if not first start: reset z = log(best_p*(1+noise)) + rng.normal*0.5
       p=softmax(z); c=conv; score=...
       U, w = soft_stats(c, tau0)
       # Adam state
       ...
       it=0; lp_counter=0; last_lp=0
       while time < substop:
          it+=1
          # check if close best: if score <= best_sc*(1-1e-12): update best
          # compute grad if time
          tau = max(1e-8, tau_base * maxc * anneal)
          U,w=soft_stats(c,tau)
          g=gradient_p(...)
          gz=p*(g-dot)
          # Adam update direction
          # line search
          ...
          if accepted:
             p=z...; c...; score...
             if score < best_sc: best...
             if improved_exact: no_improve=0 else ...
             # symm try
             # lp if it%lp_interval==0 or stuck
          else:
             no_ex +=1; lr*=0.85
             if no_ex > max_fail: break
          # anneal tau every accepted 50: if no exact improvement long, tau*=0.7 else maybe raise
    return best
```
Need choose `tau_base`. Maybe `tau = max(1e-7, 0.03 * maxc * temp)`, where `temp` from 1 down to 0.1. Or use fixed number of top K? Another robust: `tau = max(1e-6*maxc, (top min max differences?))`.   * 2: random log

- Outer while start_stop < length_deadline: next start, reset z from chosen init. This allows diversity.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_length(n, length_deadline, seed, parent):
    L=next_fast_len(2n-1)
    best_p = None; best_sc = inf
    # quick evaluate parent/interpolated to set best
    inits_specs = [] # functions that return p
    ...
    start_id=0
    while time < length_deadline - 1:
       # choose init p
       if start_id == 0:
          p0 = parent_interp or uniform
       elif start_id == 1:
          p0 = spectral
       elif start_id == 2:
          p0 = random
       elif start_id == 3:
          p0 = best_p (if exists) or random
       else:
          p0 = best_p + noise / random / cosine
       # evaluate p0, update best
       # local optimize p0 for budget start_budget
       best_p, best_sc = local_optimize(n,p0,stop_this, seed+start_id, best_p, best_sc, L, use_lp=...)
       start_id +=1
    return best_p,best_sc
```

Pseudo local Adam more concretely:
```
def _local_optimize(n, p0, stop_time, seed, L, best_p, best_score, use_lp=True):
    p = p0.copy(); p = normalize
    z = clip(log(p+1e-12), -30,30); z -= mean
    c = conv(p); score=2n*maxc
    if score<best: best...
    tau_frac=0.05; anneal=1.0
    lr = base_lr
    m=v=zeros; t=0
    fails=0; stale=0
    last_best=score
    lp_count=0
    while time<stop_time-0.2:
       if not finite... break
       maxc=c.max()
       tau = max(1e-8, tau_frac*maxc*anneal)
       U,w = soft(c,tau)
       g = grad(p,w,c,L, reg=0.05*anneal?)
       gz=p*(g - dot(g,p))
       if maxabs(gz)<1e-15: break
       t+=1; adam
       d = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       d = -d / max(abs(d))
       improved=False
       for fac in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
           nz = z + lr*fac*d  # d negative? Define d positive? Let's define direction = mhat/(...), then candidate z - lr*fac*direction
           nz = clip...
           q=softmax(nz)
           cq=conv
           sq=2n*cq.max()
           Uq,_=soft(cq,tau)
           # accept if sq < score*(1-1e-12) or (Uq < U*(1-1e-8) and sq < score*1.02)
           if accept:
               z,q,c,score,U,nz... improved=True
               if score < best_score: best...
               break
       if improved:
          fails=0
          if score < last_best - 1e-9:
             last_best=score; stale=0; lr=min(base_lr*1.05, lr*1.03)
          else:
             stale+=1
          if stale>50: anneal*=0.8; if anneal<0.05: break
          # sym every 100
          if t%100==0: check sym
          # lp every 150 if use_lp and lp_budget
       else:
          fails+=1
          lr=max(1e-4, lr*0.8)
          if fails>15: break
       anneal = max(0.05, anneal*0.999?) maybe fixed to 0.1 after 1000
    return best
```

```
def sparse_sidon_init(n, rng):
    S=[]; sums=set()
    # Try random order, maybe start with 0 and n-1?
    order=rng.permutation(n)
    for x in order:
       new_sums={2*x} | {x+s for s in S}

          S.append(x); sums |= new_sums
          if len(S)>=24: break
```

`_gradient_z(p,w,c,L,reg=0.0)`:
```
P=fft(p,L)
W=fft(w,L)
cross=ifft(P*np.conj(W)).real[:n]
g=2*cross
if reg>0:
    C=P*P # or fft(c,L)? Use P*P consistent
    crossc=ifft(P*np.conj(C)).real[:n]
    g += reg*4*crossc
dot=np.dot(g,p)
gz=p*(g-dot)
return gz
```
Need if reg uses P*P, but C can have tiny imaginary; gradient of real L2 maybe use real C? `crossc` real from ifft automatic. Since C=P*P exactly the DFT of c (with tiny imaginary), good.

```
def _optimize_length(n, stop_time, seed, parent):
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    best_p=None; best_score=inf
    # quick starts list generator
    start_id=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
       seed_r=seed*1000+start_id
       rng=np.random.default_rng(seed_r)
       if start_id==0:
          p0=_parent_to_n(parent,n,rng) if parent is not None else _uniform(n)
       elif start_id==1:
          p0=_random_dense(n,rng)
       elif start_id==2:
          p0=_spectral(n,rng)
       elif start_id==3 and best_p is not None:
          p0=best_p.copy(); p0 += small noise? maybe
       else:
          # rotate among types: best+noise, random, cosine, sparse
          typ = start_id % 5
          ...
       p0=_normalize(p0)
       sc0=_fft_score(p0,L)
       if sc0<best_score: best...
       # allocate start budget
       remaining=stop_time-now
       # budget based on n and whether exploit
       base = _start_base(n)
       budget = min(remaining-2, max(3, base*(0.75**(start_id-1 if start_id>0 else 0))))
       # For large start id, smaller
       stop_this = min(stop_time-0.5, now+budget)
       pbest, scbest = _local(n,p0,stop_this,seed_r,L,best_p,best_score,use_lp=(n<=512 and budget>10))
       if pbest is not None: best...
       start_id +=1
       if best_score < 1.49: break
    return best_p,best_score
```

```
def _parent_to_n(parent,n,rng):
   if parent.size==n: p=parent.copy()
   else:
      P=parent.size
      if P==0: return uniform
      # piecewise linear interpolation with cumulative? Since step function heights maybe length P, to length n. Use np.interp on midpoints.
      xp=(np.arange(P)+0.5)
      fp=parent
      if n==1: return [mean]
      xp_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(P/n)
      p=np.interp(xp_new, xp, fp)
   p=np.maximum(p,0); if p.sum()<0.01: p=uniform+nose; return normalize + small noise?
```

```
def _spectral_init(n,rng):
   m=2*n-1; L=next_fast_len(m)
   target=np.ones(m)/m; W=fft(target,L)
   P=np.zeros(L,complex)
   for k in range(L//2+1):
       val=W[k] if k<L else W[0]
       r=math.sqrt(abs(val))
       th=0.5*math.atan2(val.imag,val.real)
       if 0<k<L//2:
           th += math.pi if rng.random()<0.5 else 0.0
       if k==0 or (L%2==0 and k==L//2): th=0 # keep real nonnegative? phase no sign? sign could.
       P[k]=r*(math.cos(th)+1j*math.sin(th))
   for k in range(1,min(L//2,L)):
       P[L-k]=np.conj(P[k])
   p=ifft(P).real
   p=np.clip(p,0,None)
   if p.sum()<1e-12: p=rng.random(n)+0.1
   else: p=p[:n]?? Wait p length L. The inverse length L; we need length n. The flat target length m, but p should support length n. We can aggregate p into n buckets? Perhaps use p[:n]? The ifft P may have significant mass beyond n. But zero-padding in convolution assumes p length n. Taking p[:n] then normalize may not be great. Alternative choose cyclic length L=n (candidate sequence length n) and target cyclic length n? We cannot. So spectral init dubious. We can instead use `p = ifft(np.abs(W)**0.5 * random_phase)`? Not. Maybe skip spectral. Use random Fourier positive: `p = 1 + 0.5 cos(2π f x + phase)` combinations.
```

LP function:
```
def _lp_direction(p, score, n, L):
    M=score/(2*n)
    if M<=0: return None
    m=2*n-1
    A=np.empty((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
       lo=max(0,j-n+1); hi=min(n-1,j)
       A[j, lo:hi+1] = p[hi:lo-1:-1]
    A /= (M + 1e-300)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    b = np.ones(m)
    # To reduce scaling issues, maybe A=A/b? already.
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       except: return None
    if res is None or not res.success: return None
    q=np.asarray(res.x); q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return None
    q /= s
    return q
```
`bounds=(0.0,None)` works? In scipy, bounds can be scalar tuple? It accepts sequence of (min,max) or (0,None). `bounds=(0,None)` I think accepted as (min, max) for each var? If n>1, maybe (0, None) is interpreted as sequence length 2? Actually `bounds` can be (bound_min, bound_max) sequence of length 2 for all variables? In scipy, for linprog `bounds` is sequence of (min,max) or tuple of (min,max) to apply to all? I think `bounds=(0, None)` applies to all if scalar? Documentation: bounds: {sequence, (sequence, sequence), (scalar, scalar), optional}. If given (lower, upper) scalar? It might. Safer: `bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n`. For n=512 list okay. Or `bounds=(0.0, np.inf)`. Use `bounds=(0.0, None)`? To be safe: `bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n`.

Option time limit: HiGHS maybe `options={'time_limit': 2.0}`; if not recognized may error? We can omit. If LP hangs, dangerous. But size small. We can wrap in a thread? no. Could set `maxiter`? HiGHS not. We'll trust.

```
def _try_lp_update(p,score,L):
    q=_lp_direction(p,score,n,L)
    if q is None: return None,None
    best_mix=None; best_msc=inf
    for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.45,0.35,0.25,0.18,0.12,0.08,0.05):
       cand=(1-t)*p + t*q; cand=_normalize(cand)
       sc=_fft_score(cand,L)
       if sc<best_msc: best_msc=sc; best_mix=cand
    return best_mix, best_msc
```

```
def _normalize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    s=p.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones(p.size)/p.size
    return p/s
```

```
def _sq_conv(p,L):
   n=p.size
   P=fft(p,L)
   C=P*P
   c=ifft(C).real
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   c[c<0]=0
   return c
```

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is None: return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.clip(p,0,1000)
        if p.size and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum()>0: return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None
```
If parent sum 0, ignore.

`_uniform(n)`: return ones/n.
`_normalize(p)` as above.
`_log_param(p)`, `_softmax(z)`.

`_sq_conv(p,L)`: as above. Use `float` arrays. Ensure if L < 2n-1? L passed.

`_score_norm(p,L)`: c = _sq_conv; if not finite: return inf; return 2*n*float(c.max()).

`_soft(c,tau)`:
```
maxc=float(c.max())
if not finite maxc or maxc<=0: return 0, np.ones(c.size)/c.size
tau=max(1e-12, tau)
x=(c-maxc)/tau
x=np.clip(x,-50,50)
e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite:
   w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.where(c==maxc)[0]; w[idx]=1/len...
   return maxc,w
w=e/s; U=maxc+tau*np.log(s); return U,w
```
If maxc=0 (p sum0), not.

`_gradient_z(p,w,L,c=None,reg=0.0)`:
```
n=p.size
P=fft(p,L)
W=fft(w,L)
cross=ifft(P*np.conj(W)).real[:n]
g=2*cross
if reg>0:
   C=P*P
   crossc=ifft(P*np.conj(C)).real[:n]
   g += reg*4*crossc
gz=p*(g - float(np.dot(g,p)))
gz[~np.isfinite(gz)] = 0
return gz
```
Use `C=P*P` for L2. If c from P*P had clipping negative, derivative of clipped max? For L2 reg we want smooth c; using P*P before clipping okay. For objective max, we used weights over clipped c; if there are tiny negative, weights ignore. Good.

Line search `_eval_candidate(nz,n,L)`:
```
nz = nz - np.mean(nz); nz=np.clip(nz,-30,30); nz[~finite]=0
p=_softmax(nz)
c=_sq_conv(p,L)
maxc=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
score=2*n*maxc
return p,c,score
```
Maybe recompute sum? softmax sum1.

`_local(n, p0, stop, seed, L, best_p, best_score, use_lp=True)`:
We'll be careful with time. It receives limited stop. It should return best p/score (local may not update in place).
Pseudo with variables:
```
def _local(...):
    p0 = _normalize(p0)
    best = p0.copy(); bsc = _score_norm(p0,L)
    # if bsc < best_score: best_score=bsc but return best
    z0 = _log_param(p0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # run until stop? This function is called for one start, not multiple. It will use full stop for this start.
    z = z0.copy()
    p = _softmax(z); c = _sq_conv(p,L); score = _score_norm(p,L)
    if score < bsc: best=p; bsc=score
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    base_lr = ...
    lr=base_lr; anneal=1.0; tau_frac=0.08
    fails=0; stale=0; lp_every=200 if n<=256 else 400
    last_lp_iter=-10**9
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 0.1:
       if score < bsc: update
       maxc = c.max()
       tau = max(1e-10, tau_frac*max(1e-30,maxc)*anneal)
       U,w = _soft(c,tau)
       reg = 0.08*anneal if anneal>0.15 else 0.0
       gz = _gradient_z(p,w,L,c= c, reg=reg)
       if not finite or maxabs==0: break
       t+=1
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*gz
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(gz*gz)
       mhat = m/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
       direction = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       direction[~finite]=0
       dmax = np.max(np.abs(direction))
       if dmax<=0: break
       direction = -direction / dmax  # or positive? Let's define descent = direction/dmax; candidate z - lr*fac*desc? If direction = d/dmax (positive gradient directions), candidate z - lr*fac*direction.
       improved=False
       factors = (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125) if n<=512 else (1.0,0.5,0.25)
       for fac in factors:
          nz = z - lr*fac*direction
          p2,c2,s2 = _eval_candidate(nz,n,L)
          U2,_ = _soft(c2,tau)  # This is extra FFT? c2 already, soft no FFT
          s2soft=2*n*U2
          if s2 < score - 1e-12:
             accept...
          elif s2soft < 2*n*U - 1e-12 and s2 < score*1.015:
             accept...
       if improved:
          fails=0
          if score < best_score? update local best
          if bsc improved: stale=0; lr = min(base_lr*1.1, lr*1.02)
          else: stale += 1
          if stale > 60:
             anneal *= 0.7
             if anneal < 0.05: break
          if t % 100 == 0:
             # sym
             ps=0.5*(p+p[::-1]); ps=_normalize(ps); s_s=_score_norm(ps,L)
             if s_s < score -1e-12:
                p=ps; z=_log_param(p); c=_sq_conv(p,L); score=s_s; update
          # LP
          if use_lp and n<=512 and (t - last_lp_iter >= lp_every or fails>8) and time<stop-5:
             q = _lp_direction(p,score,n,L)
             if q is not None:
                # line search mixes
                best_mix=None; bm=inf
                for mix in (1.0,0.8,...):
                   cand=(1-mix)*p + mix*q; cand=_normalize(cand)
                   sc=_score_norm(cand,L)
                   if sc < bm: bm=sc; best_mix=cand
                if bm < score -1e-12:
                   p=best_mix; z=_log_param(p); c=_sq_conv(p,L); score=bm; update; m=v=0; t=0? maybe reset
                elif bm < bsc -1e-12:
                   best=best_mix; bsc=bm
                   # optionally set next start? not here
             last_lp_iter=t
       else:
          fails+=1; lr=max(1e-4, lr*0.8)
          if fails > 12: break
    return best, bsc
```

`_parent_to_n`:
```
def _parent_to_n(parent,n,rng=None):
  if parent is None or parent.size==0: return _uniform(n)
  P=parent
  if P.size==n: p=P.copy()
  else:
     x_old=(np.arange(P.size)+0.5)
     x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(P.size/n)
     p=np.interp(x_new, x_old, P)
  p=np.maximum(p,0)
  s=p.sum()
  if s<=0: return _uniform(n)
  p=p/s
  if rng is not None:
     # small noise optional? For exact parent start, no noise? We'll maybe add tiny to allow gradient.
     p = p * (1.0 + 0.02*rng.normal(0,1,n))
     p=np.clip(p,0,None); p=_normalize(p)
  return p
```
For start 0, maybe no noise? `_local` will optimize. Small noise okay. We can pass `noise=0.0`.

Random dense:
```
def _random_dense(n,rng,sigma=1.0):
  z=rng.normal(0,sigma,n); z-=z.mean(); p=_softmax(z); return p
```
Shape:
```
def _shape_init(n,rng):
   i=np.arange(n)
   p=np.ones(n)
   for _ in range(3):
      f=rng.integers(1, max(2,n//4)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); amp=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)
      p += amp*np.cos(2π f i/n + ph) + 0.3*rng.normal(0,1,n)
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-3); return _normalize(p)
```
Sparse B2:
```
def _sparse_init(n,rng,maxk=24):
   order=rng.permutation(n)
   S=[]; sums=set()
   for x in order:
       new={2*x}
       for s in S: new.add(x+s)
       if not sums.intersection(new):
           S.append(x); sums|=new
           if len(S)>=maxk: break
   if len(S)<8: return _random_dense(n,rng)
   p=np.full(n,1e-12); p[S]=1.0; return _normalize(p)
```
This greedy B2 with no swaps. Could be quick. Use n small maybe. It may produce size ~? okay.

```
  sigmas=[0.15,0.3,0.1,0.5]

     sig=0.1 + 0.2*(it%4)

     pb,sc=_local(n,pstart,now+alloc,seed+it,L,best,best_score,use_lp=True)
     if sc<bsc: best=pb; bsc=sc
     it+=1
  return best,bsc
```

```
def _optimize_length(n, stop_time, seed, parent, focus=None):
   L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   best_p=focus.copy() if focus is not None else None
   best_score = _score_norm(best_p,L) if best_p is not None else inf
   if best_p is None:
      p=_parent_to_n(parent,n); best_p=p; best_score=_score
   T=stop_time-time-2
   if T<2: return best_p,best_score
   if n<=128: K=min(15,max(3,int(T/15)))
   elif n<=256: K=min(10,max(3,int(T/45)))
   elif n<=512: K=min(6,max(2,int(T/70)))
   else: K=min(4,max(2,int(T/110)))
   time_per=max(3,T/K)
   start=0
   while time<stop_time-2:
       remaining=stop_time-time
       if remaining <=2: break
       if start==0 and focus is not None:
           p0=focus.copy()
           # maybe tiny noise
       elif start==0:
           p0=_parent_to_n(parent,n)
       else:
           typ = start if focus is None else (start-1)
           if focus is not None and start==1:
               p0=focus * exp(noise 0.15)
           else:
             typ = typ % 6
             if typ==0: p0=_parent_to_n(parent,n,noise=True)
             elif typ==1: p0=_random_dense(n,rng,sigma=1.5)
             elif typ==2: p0=_shape_init(n,rng)
             elif typ==3: p0=_sparse_init(n,rng)
             elif typ==4 and best_p is not None:
                 p0=best_p * exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,n)); normalize
             else: p0=_random_dense
       p0=_normalize(p0); sc0=_score_norm(p0,L); update best if better
       alloc=min(time_per* (1.4 if start==0 else 1.0), remaining-2)
       stop_alloc=min(stop_time-0.5, time+alloc)
       pb,scb=_local(n,p0,stop_alloc,seed+1000*start+ n, L,best_p,best_score, use_lp=(n<=512 and alloc>8))
       if pb is not None and scb<best_score: best=pb; best_score=scb
       start+=1
       if best_score < 1.49: break
   return best_p,best_score
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
        if h is None:
            return None
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            return None
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.sum() <= 0:
            return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(p, n=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
       return np.array([1.0])
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)/p.size
    return p/s
```
For internal n, size fixed.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0))
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
```

`_log_param`:
```
def _log_param(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,1e-300,1000)
   z=np.log(p); z-=np.mean(z); z=np.clip(z,-30,30); z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0); return z
```

`_sq_conv`:
```
def _sq_conv(p,L):
    n = p.size
    m = 2*n - 1
    if L < m: L = next_fast_len(m)
    P = fft(p, L)
    C = P * P
    c = ifft(C).real[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c, L
```
Return L maybe same. In functions, L passed fixed. If we return new L, ensure consistent. Better not adjust internally; pass correct L. Remove L return.

```
def _score_norm(p,L):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0: return math.inf
    if abs(s-1)>1e-9: p=p/s; n=p.size # but L maybe based on old n? If n same. We'll assume normalized.
    c=_sq_conv(p,L)
    if c.size ==0: return math.inf
    mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return math.inf
    return 2.0*n*mx
```
If p not normalized but same length, rescale. If L based on n, ok.

`_soft`:
```
def _soft(c,tau):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size==0: return 0.0, np.array([0.0])
    mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0:
       w=np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size)
       return 0.0,w
    tau=max(1e-12,float(tau))
    x=(c-mx)/tau
    x=np.clip(x,-50.0,50.0)
    e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       idx=np.where(c>=mx - 1e-12*max(1,mx))[0]
       if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=1.0/idx.size
       return mx,w
    w=e/s
    U=mx + tau*math.log(s)
    if not np.isfinite(U): U=mx
    return float(U), w
```

`_gradient_z`:
```
def _gradient_z(p,w,L,reg=0.0):
    n=p.size
    P=fft(p,L)
    W=fft(w,L)
    cross=ifft(P*np.conj(W)).real[:n]
    g=2.0*cross
    if reg>0:
       C=P*P
       crossc=ifft(P*np.conj(C)).real[:n]
       g = g + reg*(4.0*crossc)
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
    dot=float(np.dot(g,p))
    gz=p*(g-dot)
    gz=np.where(np.isfinite(gz),gz,0.0)
    return gz
```
Potential: `fft(w,L)` where w length m<L zero-padded automatically? If w length exactly m, `fft(w, L)` zero pads. good.

```
def _candidate_from_z(z,n,L):
    nz=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
    nz=np.where(np.isfinite(nz),nz,0.0)
    nz -= np.mean(nz)
    nz=np.clip(nz,-30,30)
    p=_softmax(nz)
    c=_sq_conv(p,L)
    mx=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
    if not np.isfinite(mx): mx=0
    score=2.0*n*mx
    return p,c,score
```

LP:
```
def _lp_direction(p,score,n):
    if linprog is None: return None
    M=score/(2.0*n)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-15: return None
    m=2*n-1
    A=np.empty((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
       lo=max(0,j-n+1); hi=min(n-1,j)
       if hi>=lo:
          A[j, lo:hi+1] = p[np.arange(hi, lo-1, -1)]
    A /= (M + 1e-300)
    A=np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.ones(m)
    # scale objective? no
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n, method='highs-ds')
       except Exception:
          return None
    if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False):
       return None
    q=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q=np.where(np.isfinite(q),q,0.0)
    q=np.clip(q,0,None)
    s=q.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return None
    return q/s
```
Could be slow. We can add if n>512 not called.

Now, `_parent_to_n`: ensure output length n. If interpolation p has tiny negative due to parent? clip.
```
def _parent_to_n(parent,n,noise=0.0,rng=None,use_parent=True):
   if n <=0: return np.ones(1)
   if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
      P=parent.astype(float)
      if P.size == n: p=P.copy()
      else:
         x_old=(np.arange(P.size)+0.5)
         x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(P.size/n)
         p=np.interp(x_new, x_old, P)
      p=np.clip(p,0,None)
      s=p.sum()
      if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)
      else: p=p/s
   else:
      p=np.ones(n)/n
   if rng is not None and noise>0:
      p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise, n))
      p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s>0: p=p/s
   return p
```
Use noise=0.0 for exact; 0.1 for variants.

```
def _optimize_length(n, stop_time, seed, parent, focus=None):
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    if focus is not None:
       p=focus.copy(); p=_normalize(p,c)? ensure len n
       best_p=p; best_score=_score_norm(p,L)
    else:
       p=_parent_to_n(parent,n); best_p=p; best_score=_score_norm(p,L)
    # quick initial best
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total=stop_time - time.monotonic() - 2.0
    if total < 3: return best_p,best_score
    # K starts
    if n<=64: K=min(16, max(4, int(total/12)))
    elif n<=128: K=min(12, max(3, int(total/25)))
    elif n<=256: K=min(10, max(3, int(total/45)))
    elif n<=512: K=min(7, max(2, int(total/70)))
    else: K=min(5, max(2, int(total/110)))
    per = total / K
    start=0
    while time < stop_time - 2:
        rem=stop_time-time
        if rem<3: break
        if focus is not None and start==0:
            p0=focus.copy()
            # slight noise? For first focus exact; local will move.
        else:
            # choose type
            if start==0 and focus is None:
                p0=_parent_to_n(parent,n,0.0,rng)
            else:
                typ = start % 6
                if typ==0:
                    p0=_parent_to_n(parent,n,0.15,rng)
                elif typ==1:
                    p0=_random_dense(n,rng,1.2)
                elif typ==2:
                    p0=_shape_init(n,rng)
                elif typ==3:
                    p0=_sparse_init(n,rng)
                elif typ==4 and best_p is not None:
                    p0=best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25,n))
                    p0=_normalize(p0)
                else:
                    p0=_random_dense(n,rng,2.0)
        p0=_normalize(p0)
        if p0.size != n:
            # fallback
            p0=_uniform(n)
        sc0=_score_norm(p0,L)
        if sc0 < best_score:
            best_score=sc0; best_p=p0.copy()
        alloc = min(rem-2, per*(1.3 if (start==0 or focus is not None and start<2) else 1.0))
        alloc = max(2.0, alloc)
        stop_alloc=min(stop_time-0.5, time.monotonic()+alloc)
        pb,scb=_local(n,p0,stop_alloc,seed+1234*start+n,L,best_p,best_score, use_lp=(n<=512 and alloc>6))
        if pb is not None:
            pb=_normalize(pb)
            if pb.size==n and np.isfinite(pb).all():
                scb=_score_norm(pb,L)
                if scb < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best_score=scb; best_p=pb.copy()
        start += 1
        if best_score < 1.49:
            break
    return best_p,best_score
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is None:
            return None
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            return None
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.sum() <= 0.0:
            return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None


def _uniform(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
    return p / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _log_param(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-300, 1000.0)
    z = np.log(p)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    return z


def _sq_conv(p, L):
    n = int(p.size)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if int(L) < m:
        L = next_fast_len(m)
    P = fft(p, L)
    c = ifft(P * P).real[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c, int(L)


def _score_norm(p, L):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return math.inf
    p = p / s
    c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def _soft_max(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    tau = max(1e-12, float(tau))
    x = (c - mx) / tau
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = np.where(c >= mx - 1e-12 * max(1.0, mx))[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return mx, w
    w = e / s
    U = mx + tau * math.log(s)
    if not np.isfinite(U):
        U = mx
    return float(U), w


def _gradient_z(p, w, L, reg=0.0):
    n = int(p.size)
    P = fft(p, L)
    W = fft(w, L)
    cross = ifft(P * np.conj(W)).real[:n]
    g = 2.0 * cross
    if reg > 0.0:
        C = P * P
        crossc = ifft(P * np.conj(C)).real[:n]
        g = g + reg * (4.0 * crossc)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(g, p))
    gz = p * (g - dot)
    gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
    return gz


def _candidate_from_z(z, n, L):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    p = _softmax(z)
    c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * mx
    return p, c, score


def _parent_to_n(parent, n, noise=0.0, rng=None):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
        return _uniform(n)
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if parent.size == n:
        p = parent.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(parent.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
        x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) * float(parent.size) / float(n)
        p = np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s
    if rng is not None and noise > 0.0:
        p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / s
    return p


def _random_dense(n, rng, sigma=1.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    z = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return _softmax(z)


def _shape_init(n, rng):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(3):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 4)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.85))
        p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * i / float(n) + ph)
        p += 0.25 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-3)
    return _normalize(p)


def _sparse_init(n, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    maxk = int(math.sqrt(2.0 * n)) + 4
    maxk = min(max(10, maxk), 48)
    order = rng.permutation(n)
    S = []
    sums = set()
    for x in order:
        xi = int(x)
        new = {2 * xi}
        for y in S:
            new.add(xi + y)
        if sums.intersection(new):
            continue
        S.append(xi)
        sums.update(new)
        if len(S) >= maxk:
            break
    if len(S) < 8:
        return _random_dense(n, rng, 1.0)
    p = np.full(n, 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
    p[S] = 1.0
    return _normalize(p)


def _lp_direction(p, score, n):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    M = float(score) / (2.0 * float(n))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-15:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.empty((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
        lo = max(0, j - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, j)
        if hi >= lo:
            A[j, lo:hi + 1] = p[np.arange(hi, lo - 1, -1)]
    A /= (M + 1e-300)
    A = np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    res = None
    for method in (None, "highs", "highs-ds"):
        for opts in ({"time_limit": 3.0, "presolve": True}, {"presolve": True}, {}):
            try:
                if method is None:
                    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
                else:
                    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method=method, options=opts)
                if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
                    break
            except Exception:
                res = None
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
            break
    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    q = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return q / s


def _local_optime(n, p0, stop_time, seed, L, use_lp=True):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    p0 = _normalize(p0)
    if p0.size != n:
        p0 = _uniform(n)

    c, L = _sq_conv(p0, L)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) if c.size else math.inf
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        p0 = _uniform(n)
        c, L = _sq_conv(p0, L)
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) if c.size else math.inf

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = score
    z = _log_param(p0)
    p = p0.copy()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if n <= 32:
        base_lr = 0.9
    elif n <= 128:
        base_lr = 0.55
    elif n <= 512:
        base_lr = 0.35
    elif n <= 1024:
        base_lr = 0.25
    else:
        base_lr = 0.16

    lr = base_lr
    tau_frac = 0.14
    anneal = 1.0
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1, beta2, eps = 0.90, 0.999, 1e-8
    t = 0
    fails = 0
    stale = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    max_lp = 8 if n <= 256 else (4 if n <= 512 else 0)
    lp_interval = 120 if n <= 256 else 300

    if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 0.2:
        return best_p, best_score

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 0.15:
        if not np.isfinite(score) or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            break
        if score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            stale = 0

        if best_score <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
            break

        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = tau_frac * maxc * anneal
        tau = max(1e-12, tau)
        U, w = _soft_max(c, tau)
        reg = 0.10 * min(1.0, anneal)
        gz = _gradient_z(p, w, L, reg=reg)
        gnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(gz))) if gz.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm <= 1e-16:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(z)
            c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) if c.size else math.inf
            m = np.zeros(n)
            v = np.zeros(n)
            t = 0
            continues = True
            continue

        t += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * gz
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (gz * gz)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)
        direction = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        direction = np.where(np.isfinite(direction), direction, 0.0)
        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(direction))) if direction.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-18:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(z)
            c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) if c.size else math.inf
            m = np.zeros(n)
            v = np.zeros(n)
            t = 0
            continue
        direction = direction / dmax

        improved = False
        factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125) if n <= 1024 else (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        cur_score = score
        cur_soft = 2.0 * float(n) * U
        for fac in factors:
            nz = z - lr * fac * direction
            p2, c2, s2 = _candidate_from_z(nz, n, L)
            if not np.isfinite(s2):
                continue
            if s2 < cur_score - 1e-13:
                improved = True
                z, p, c, score, cur_score = nz, p2, c2, s2, s2
                break
            if s2 <= cur_score * 1.02:
                U2, _ = _soft_max(c2, tau)
                s2_soft = 2.0 * float(n) * U2
                if s2_soft < cur_soft - 1e-12:
                    improved = True
                    z, p, c, score, cur_score = nz, p2, c2, s2, s2
                    break

        if improved:
            fails = 0
            if score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                stale = 0
            else:
                stale += 1
            if stale > 90:
                break
            lr = min(base_lr * 1.25, lr * 1.03)
            anneal = max(0.05, anneal * (0.999 if stale == 0 else 0.996))

            if t % 120 == 0:
                ps = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
                ps = _normalize(ps)
                cs = _score_norm(ps, L)
                if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < score - 1e-12:
                    p = ps
                    z = _log_param(p)
                    c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
                    score = cs
                    m *= 0.5
                    v *= 0.5

            if (
                use_lp
                and max_lp > 0
                and lp_calls < max_lp
                and (t > 25 and (t % lp_interval == 0 or fails > 6))
                and time.monotonic() < stop_time - 6.0
            ):
                q = _lp_direction(p, score, n)
                lp_calls += 1
                if q is not None:
                    best_mix = None
                    bm = math.inf
                    for mix in (1.0, 0.8, 0.65, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.12):
                        cand = (1.0 - mix) * p + mix * q
                        cand = _normalize(cand)
                        sc = _score_norm(cand, L)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bm:
                            bm = sc
                            best_mix = cand
                    if best_mix is not None and np.isfinite(bm):
                        if bm < score - 1e-12:
                            p = best_mix
                            z = _log_param(p)
                            c, L = _sq_conv(p, L)
                            score = bm
                            best_score = bm
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            m = np.zeros(n)
                            v = np.zeros(n)
                            t = 0
                            lr = base_lr
                            anneal = 1.0
                            stale = 0
                            fails = 0
                        elif bm < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = bm
                            best_p = best_mix.copy()
        else:
            fails += 1
            lr = max(1e-4, lr * 0.82)
            if fails > 12 or stale > 130:
                break

    return best_p, best_score


def _optimize_length(n, stop_time, seed, parent):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    p0 = _parent_to_n(parent, n, 0.0, np.random.default_rng(seed))
    best_p = p0
    best_score = _score_norm(best_p, L)
    T = max(0.0, float(stop_time) - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if T < 3.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    if n <= 64:
        K = int(min(16, max(4, T / 12.0)))
    elif n <= 128:
        K = int(min(12, max(3, T / 25.0)))
    elif n <= 256:
        K = int(min(10, max(3, T / 45.0)))
    elif n <= 512:
        K = int(min(7, max(2, T / 70.0)))
    else:
        K = int(min(5, max(2, T / 110.0)))
    per = T / max(1, K)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = 0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
        rem = stop_time - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 2.5:
            break
        if start == 0:
            pstart = _parent_to_n(parent, n, 0.0, rng)
        else:
            typ = start % 6
            if typ == 0:
                pstart = _parent_to_n(parent, n, 0.18, rng)
            elif typ == 1:
                pstart = _random_dense(n, rng, 1.2)
            elif typ == 2:
                pstart = _shape_init(n, rng)
            elif typ == 3:
                pstart = _sparse_init(n, rng) if n <= 1024 else _shape_init(n, rng)
            elif typ == 4:
                z = _log_param(best_p)
                z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.30, n)
                pstart = _softmax(z)
                pstart = _normalize(0.7 * pstart + 0.3 * _parent_to_n(parent, n, 0.05, rng))
            else:
                pstart = _random_dense(n, rng, 2.0)

        pstart = _normalize(pstart)
        if pstart.size != n:
            pstart = _uniform(n)
        sc0 = _score_norm(pstart, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc0
            best_p = pstart.copy()

        alloc = min(rem - 2.0, per * (1.35 if start == 0 else 1.0))
        alloc = max(2.0, alloc)
        stop_this = min(stop_time - 0.5, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        pb, scb = _local_optime(n, pstart, stop_this, seed + 1000 * start + 7 * n, L, use_lp=(n <= 512 and alloc > 6.0))
        if pb is not None:
            pb = _normalize(pb)
            if pb.size == n:
                scb = _score_norm(pb, L)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best_score = scb
                    best_p = pb.copy()
        start += 1
        if best_score < 1.49:
            break

    return best_p, best_score


def _focus_optimization(n, p0, stop_time, seed, parent):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    p0 = _normalize(p0)
    if p0.size != n:
        p0 = _uniform(n)
    L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(best_p, L)
    T = max(0.0, float(stop_time) - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
    if T < 4.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    K = int(min(8, max(2, T / 28.0)))
    per = T / max(1, K)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = 0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 2.0:
        rem = stop_time - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 2.5:
            break
        if start == 0:
            pstart = best_p.copy()
        else:
            sig = 0.08 + 0.07 * (start % 6)
            z = _log_param(best_p)
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            pstart = _softmax(z)
            if start % 4 == 2:
                pp = _parent_to_n(parent, n, 0.10, rng)
                pstart = _normalize(0.7 * pstart + 0.3 * pp)
        sc0 = _score_norm(pstart, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc0
            best_p = pstart.copy()

        alloc = min(rem - 2.0, per * (1.25 if start == 0 else 1.0))
        alloc = max(2.0, alloc)
        stop_this = min(stop_time - 0.5, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        pb, scb = _local_optime(n, pstart, stop_this, seed + 9000 * start + 11 * n, L, use_lp=(n <= 512 and alloc > 6.0))
        if pb is not None:
            pb = _normalize(pb)
            if pb.size == n:
                scb = _score_norm(pb, L)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best_score = scb
                    best_p = pb.copy()
        start += 1
        if best_score < 1.49:
            break

    return best_p, best_score


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        evalf = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
        if evalf is None:
            return math.inf
        return float(evalf([float(x) for x in seq]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _safe_list(arr, default_len=32):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(int(default_len), dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(int(default_len), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    budget = min(float(budget), 1300.0)

    parent = _get_parent()
    if parent is None:
        fallback = _uniform(64)
    else:
        fallback = _normalize(parent)

    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_score = math.inf
    try:
        L0 = next_fast_len(2 * best_seq.size - 1)
        best_score = _score_norm(best_seq, L0)
    except Exception:
        best_score = 2.0

    if budget < 35.0:
        return _safe_list(best_seq)

    deadline = t0 + max(8.0, budget - 12.0)
    plen = int(fallback.size) if fallback.size > 0 else 192

    raw_lengths = [max(8, plen)]
    if plen > 1:
        for f in (2.0, 3.0, 0.5, 1.5, 4.0, 0.25, 2.5, 0.75):
            L = int(round(plen * f))
            if L >= 8 and L <= 4096:
                raw_lengths.append(L)
    raw_lengths.extend([16, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096])

    seen = set()
    lengths = []
    for L in raw_lengths:
        L = int(L)
        if 8 <= L <= 4096 and L not in seen:
            seen.add(L)
            lengths.append(L)

    def _prio(L):
        if L == plen:
            return 100000
        d = abs(math.log(max(1, L)) - math.log(max(1, plen)))
        score = -12.0 * d
        if 96 <= L <= 1024:
            score += 90
        elif 32 <= L < 96 or 1024 < L <= 2048:
            score += 30
        elif L > 2048:
            score -= 40
        elif L < 32:
            score -= 25
        return score

    lengths.sort(key=_prio, reverse=True)
    lengths = lengths[:9]

    total_available = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
    if total_available > 20.0:
        wsum = []
        for i, L in enumerate(lengths):
            if i == 0:
                w = 0.36
            elif i == 1:
                w = 0.24
            elif i == 2:
                w = 0.14
            elif i == 3:
                w = 0.08
            else:
                w = 0.05
            if L > 2048:
                w *= 0.35
            if L < 32:
                w *= 0.55
            wsum.append(w)
        s = float(sum(wsum))
        if s <= 0.0:
            wsum = [1.0] * len(lengths)
            s = float(sum(wsum))

        for L, w in zip(lengths, wsum):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break
            best_score < 1.49
            alloc = (w / s) * total_available
            if alloc < 8.0:
                continue
            stop_length = min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
            try:
                cand, sc = _optimize_length(L, stop_length, int(seed) + 100003 + 17 * L, parent)
            except Exception:
                cand = None
                sc = math.inf
            if cand is not None:
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                if cand.size == L:
                    try:
                        LL = next_fast_len(2 * cand.size - 1)
                        sc2 = _score_norm(cand, LL)
                    except Exception:
                        sc2 = sc
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_seq = cand.copy()
                        if best_score < 1.49:
                            break

    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size >= 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0)
        if remaining > 20.0:
            focus_stop = min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + max(8.0, remaining * 0.85))
            try:
                cand, sc = _focus_optimization(best_seq.size, best_seq, focus_stop, int(seed) + 987654, parent)
            except Exception:
                cand = None
                sc = math.inf
            if cand is not None:
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                try:
                    LL = next_fast_len(2 * cand.size - 1)
                    sc2 = _score_norm(cand, LL)
                except Exception:
                    sc2 = sc
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = cand.copy()

    candidates = [best_seq, fallback]
    best_list = None
    best_exact = math.inf
    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            lst = _safe_list(cand)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not lst:
            continue
        try:
            esc = _exact_score(lst)
        except Exception:
            esc = math.inf
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_exact - 1e-12:
            best_exact = esc
            best_list = lst

    if best_list is None:
        best_list = _safe_list(fallback)
    return best_list
```
